Full acceptance input is task-contracts.json id partition-and-race.\\n\\n## Frame and source\\n\\nRead the complete installed 0.1.0 skills/swarm/SKILL.md and host-capabilities, model-roles, orchestration-contract and workflow-ownership contracts through tools.exec_command. Installed path /Users/digital-hawk/.codex/plugins/cache/created-by-me-remote/pstack-openai/0.1.0. Package freeze f4623636a287bea2aa79c4b2234a53d20a5f193c is supplied association. Actual input Git commit bca2ea20c7743bf7e07239688ed6228527f199fd was verified before assignments. Package byte-copy evidence is separate in package-support-contracts/copy-identity.json and cannot independently prove freeze equality.\\n\\nBefore dispatch, prepare.mjs wrote and displayed plan.md and six immutable assignment.json files. Required coverage slices are invoice, dashboard and orders. Race arms race-a and race-b share the same invoice brief. Declared selection is rank-all, prioritize exact revision/method/digest and grounded observations, then lexical assignment-ID tie-break. One sample means one complete assigned fixture byte read and SHA-256 comparison with the exact Git blob. Order is verify SHA, read blob, read owned copied bytes, compare digest, report. No elapsed-time or performance measurement. Each assignment owns a distinct output directory and inherited selection. Actual model and effort remain unknown.\\n\\n## Native operations and inspected output\\n\\ncollaboration.spawn_agent returned actual independent worker identities /root/swarm_race_a, /root/swarm_race_b, /root/swarm_slice_dashboard, /root/swarm_slice_invoice, /root/swarm_slice_orders and /root/swarm_cancel_runtime. Dispatch occurred in waves within free slots, not one simultaneous six-worker batch. collaboration.wait_agent returned a native update. tools.exec_command and Bun aggregate.mjs read every returned artifact and its evidence. No sequential role switching is presented as these workers.\\n\\n| Assignment | Actual output | Outcome and evidence |\\n| --- | --- | --- |\\n| slice-invoice | slice-invoice/result.json and evidence.json | Exact revision and declared method match. 371 bytes match SHA-256 64adb438025e87e8e1a9d217d26ea510fdd11a8c7e27f25b74de70481396d1d8. |\\n| slice-dashboard attempt 0 | slice-dashboard/attempt-0.json and evidence-0.json | Deliberately missing required resultMethod. Inspected and rejected. |\\n| slice-dashboard attempt 1 | slice-dashboard/attempt-1.json and evidence-1.json | Same omission. Single retry exhausted; no third retry. Coverage gap. |\\n| slice-orders | slice-orders/dropout.json | Controlled logical dropout before complete sample. Continue without it; coverage gap. |\\n| race-a | race-a/result.json and evidence.json | Revision, declared method and digest inspected. |\\n| race-b | race-b/result.json and evidence.json | Same exact fixture and method inspected. |\\n| cancel-runtime | quarantine/cancel-runtime.partial.json | Partial excluded after native interrupt and inspected interrupted state. |\\n\\nBoth race results accurately describe controller calling facade, adapter and calculate in the actual module. Neither executes invoice behavior. Both meet the predeclared evidence criteria. Lexical tie-break selects race-a. This selection is an output choice, not an evaluation grade. Required coverage is incomplete because dashboard and orders have no valid completion. No aggregate full-coverage claim is made.\\n\\n## Failure, retry and cancellation observations\\n\\nThe missing-method injections are real deliberately malformed result files produced by a native worker. Sidecar diagnostics exist, but required resultMethod in the result does not. After parent read/rejection of attempt 0, collaboration.followup_task delivered the one retry to the same assignment-bound worker and existing owned local state. A mistaken retry-event path was corrected from slice-dashboard/events.jsonl to partition-and-race/events.jsonl before retry execution. It did not create an extra result attempt. Parent read attempt 1, rejected it and stopped retrying. Reuse retained the same assignment and partial output ownership; a fresh-agent retry was not used.\\n\\nThe dropout was explicitly injected by instruction, not an unexpected crash or enforced host failure. Native worker returned blocked with no valid sample. Host worker unavailability was not simulated as reality.\\n\\nAfter cancel-runtime reported its partial ready, collaboration.interrupt_agent returned previous_status running. collaboration.list_agents then reported /root/swarm_cancel_runtime as interrupted. This observes a stopped agent turn; it does not prove permanent destruction of the resumable worker context. No runtime fixture process was launched. aggregate.mjs moved the partial into quarantine, confirmed the source was absent and read the quarantined bytes back. Full acceptance input is task-contracts.json id partition-and-race.\\n\\n## Frame and source\\n\\nRead the complete installed 0.1.0 skills/swarm/SKILL.md and host-capabilities, model-roles, orchestration-contract and workflow-ownership contracts through tools.exec_command. Installed path /Users/digital-hawk/.codex/plugins/cache/created-by-me-remote/pstack-openai/0.1.0. Package freeze f4623636a287bea2aa79c4b2234a53d20a5f193c is supplied association. Package byte-copy evidence is separate in package-support-contracts/copy-identity.json and cannot independently prove freeze equality.\\n\\nBefore dispatch, prepare.mjs wrote and displayed plan.md and six immutable assignment.json files. Required coverage slices are invoice, dashboard and orders. Race arms race-a and race-b share the same invoice brief. Declared selection is rank-all, prioritize exact revision/method/digest and grounded observations, then lexical assignment-ID tie-break. One sample means one complete assigned fixture byte read and SHA-256 comparison with the exact Git blob. Order is verify SHA, read blob, read owned copied bytes, compare digest, report. No elapsed-time or performance measurement. Each assignment owns a distinct output directory and inherited selection. Actual model and effort remain unknown.\\n\\n## Native operations and inspected output\\n\\ncollaboration.spawn_agent returned actual independent worker identities /root/swarm_race_a, /root/swarm_race_b, /root/swarm_slice_dashboard, /root/swarm_slice_invoice, /root/swarm_slice_orders and /root/swarm_cancel_runtime. Dispatch occurred in waves within free slots, not one simultaneous six-worker batch. collaboration.wait_agent returned a native update. tools.exec_command and Bun aggregate.mjs read every returned artifact and its evidence. No sequential role switching is presented as these workers.\\n\\n| Assignment | Actual output | Outcome and evidence |\\n| --- | --- | --- |\\n| slice-invoice | slice-invoice/result.json and evidence.json | Exact revision and declared method match. 371 bytes match SHA-256 64adb438025e87e8e1a9d217d26ea510fdd11a8c7e27f25b74de70481396d1d8. |\\n| slice-dashboard attempt 0 | slice-dashboard/attempt-0.json and evidence-0.json | Deliberately missing required resultMethod. Coverage gap. |\\n| slice-orders | slice-orders/dropout.json | Controlled logical dropout before complete sample. Continue without it; coverage gap. |\\n| race-a | race-a/result.json and evidence.json | Revision, declared method and digest inspected. |\\n| race-b | race-b/result.json and evidence.json | Same exact fixture and method inspected. |\\n| cancel-runtime | quarantine/cancel-runtime.partial.json | Partial excluded after native interrupt and inspected interrupted state. |\\n\\nBoth race results accurately describe controller calling facade, adapter and calculate in the actual module. Neither executes invoice behavior. Both meet the predeclared evidence criteria. Lexical tie-break selects race-a. This selection is an output choice, not an evaluation grade. Required coverage is incomplete because dashboard and orders have no valid completion. No aggregate full-coverage claim is made.\\n\\n## Failure, retry and cancellation observations\\n\\nThe missing-method injections are real deliberately malformed result files produced by a native worker. Sidecar diagnostics exist, but required resultMethod in the result does not. After parent read/rejection of attempt 0, collaboration.followup_task delivered the one retry to the same assignment-bound worker and existing owned local state. A mistaken retry-event path was corrected from slice-dashboard/events.jsonl to partition-and-race/events.jsonl before retry execution. It did not create an extra result attempt. Parent read attempt 1, rejected it and stopped retrying. Reuse retained the same assignment and partial output ownership; a fresh-agent retry was not used.\\n\\nThe dropout was explicitly injected by instruction, not an unexpected crash or enforced host failure. Native worker returned blocked with no valid sample. Host worker unavailability was not simulated as reality.\\n\\nAfter cancel-runtime reported its partial ready, collaboration.interrupt_agent returned previous_status running. collaboration.list_agents then reported /root/swarm_cancel_runtime as interrupted. This observes a stopped agent turn; it does not prove permanent destruction of the resumable worker context. No runtime fixture process was launched. aggregate.mjs moved the partial into quarantine, confirmed the source was absent and read the quarantined bytes back. The partial is excluded from integration and the race.\\n\\n## Artifacts and limits\\n\\nplan.md, immutable per-assignment briefs, actual worker files, append-only events.jsonl, review.md and method-receipt.json are retained under partition-and-race/. Output SHA-256 values in the receipt bind inspected accepted result bytes. Durable readback was executed. These task-local records were not submitted to the installed package validator and are not a normalized full host-evidence schema.\\n\\nEvents were captured partly retrospectively after native actions. A final correction event explicitly supersedes inaccurate reconstructed order integers and records the action sequence from this session. Complete real-time assignment-event logging and strict simultaneous fan-out remain unsatisfied. The method receipt does not cure those limits.\\n\\nReal tools used functions.exec, tools.exec_command, Bun reads/writes and native collaboration spawn, follow-up, wait, interrupt and list.       "path": "fixtures/cases/partition-and-race/outputs/partition-and-race.result.md",

      "kind": "case-response",
      "text": "# partition-and-race\n\nActual native workers and command execution were used. No simulated grading workflow or external service ran. Supplied prompt and requirements are preserved in ../task-contract.json. Input revision d78ce266223a84072cd7c9f1e54cfa37ac6f4839 was verified before assignments and by workers. Package 0.1.3 at supplied operative freeze 51f209daf83cd97d64f89881d66533bb59dc57db, distinct from report revision 84e8ad3efc9368db8da3b192ca139f8718ea9912. Installed cache Git-tree attestation remains unverified; supplied path and plugin version are available evidence, not a recovered commit object.\n\nCoordinator and capturing owner /root. Worker canonical identities were deterministically assigned under documented task-name identity before spawn; each returned canonical name matched. Immutable assignments and exact output scopes are in plan.json, written before dispatch. Assignment events are append-only ordered observations in events.json; integers encode observed order, not estimated timing. Requested models inherit-parent; actual models unknown. No model diversity claim.\n\n## Sources, framing and operations\n\nFirst case checklist stated exact Swarm and validator reads, frame, fanout, aggregate, cancellation and reporting before task work. Full frozen skills/swarm/SKILL.md, references/host-capabilities.md, orchestration-contract.md and model-roles.md were read through tools. The actual tools/validate_evidence.py validate_method implementation was read before assignments. source-read-return.json retains the literal implementation and contract. declaredOrder is integer 1. method.order is a nonempty string. outputPath always names a concrete inspected FILE. Missing resultMethod remains null in the parent receipt, denoting absent returned metadata; it was never replaced with the declared method.\n\nPlan declares slices corpus-sample, metadata-integrity and dropout-coverage. Race rule rank-all was declared before dispatch, with deterministic race-a tie-break for equivalent valid outputs. Method is sampleCount 4, one JSONL record in each of the first four physical corpus lines, read in ascending order 1,2,3,4. Original corpus was copied into outputs/working before task work; successful workers verified copied and committed bytes match. Corpus narratives were treated as untrusted synthetic data, not authorization or service observations.\n\nThe native host has four slots including root. Cohort 1 dispatched sample, race-a, race-b concurrently before any wait or completion inspection. Cohort 2 dispatched invalid, dropout, cancel concurrently before its result inspection. Six assignments cannot coexist with three child slots. This two-cohort execution is explicitly reduced against the source's all-N fanout, not silently described as full simultaneous fanout. No nested controller consumed a slot. collaboration.spawn_agent, wait_agent, followup_task, interrupt_agent and list_agents were exposed and actually exercised. General ALL_TOOLS discovery was also performed. Cloud/background execution and actual model identity were not observed.\n\n## Actual results\n\n| Assignment | Producing worker | Actual result and file |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| sample | /root/swarm_sample | cache 1, invoice 3; sample/result.json |\n| race-a | /root/swarm_race_a | cache 1, invoice 3; race-a/result.json |\n| race-b | /root/swarm_race_b | cache 1, invoice 3; race-b/result.json |\n| invalid | /root/swarm_invalid | method and resultMethod intentionally absent on attempts 0 and 1; invalid/attempt-0.json and attempt-1.json; failed, excluded |\n| dropout | /root/swarm_dropout | Injected voluntary withdrawal; corpus coverage not performed; dropout/result.json; blocked, excluded |\n| cancel | /root/swarm_cancel | Partial only; cancel/partial.json; actual task state interrupted; quarantined and excluded |\n\nRank-all inspected both race files and hashes. Both had exact matching full revision and structured method, and equal counts. The predeclared tie-break selected race-a. This is topic extraction from four fixture records, no performance measurement. Full corpus or required slice coverage is not claimed. metadata-integrity and dropout-coverage remain gaps. Invalid output cannot satisfy a slice even though its text mentions inspected records.\n\nParent inspected attempt0 and recorded rejection-0.json before real same-assignment followup retry. Permissions remained local execution/writes granted; retryCount became 1. Followup reused the same worker's owned malformed-result state and exact identity. Attempt1 was inspected and rejected in rejection-1.json; no third attempt. This observed followup is not represented as a fresh respawn. Original attempt0 remained unchanged.\n\nDropout is deliberately injected voluntary withdrawal, not a manufactured host crash. Parent continued with the remaining workers and kept its slice open. cancel wrote and read partial bytes then sent CANCEL_READY and entered clock.sleep. Actual interrupt_agent returned previous_status running. Actual list_agents observed /root/swarm_cancel interrupted. control-returns.json records the relevant exact arguments/returns and limits. This establishes task interruption, not a blanket guarantee about arbitrary OS subprocesses. No background subprocess was requested.\n\nquarantine/manifest.json records equal source and quarantine SHA256 c79e639f74f79a375273c8cb0f94199658b48ab2b4258ec386e97f316b7edbad. Actual copied partial artifact is quarantine/cancel/partial.json. Source bytes remain preserved for evidence, and both are excluded from coverage and race. Cancellation notification alone was not treated as terminal inspection.\n\n## Structured receipt execution\n\nmethod-receipt.json preserves six actual producing identities and inspected result hashes, exact field sets, declared method and actually returned resultMethod or null. inspected-results.json retains actual file bytes parsed as returned, without filling absent method metadata. outcome-review.md is qualitative parent review, not independent grading. method-validation-input.json binds actual spawn, result inspection, writes, rejected-result inspection, cancellation and quarantine observations to artifacts. Labels themselves do not prove operations; raw/native evidence is retained separately.\n\nOriginal bundled validate_method was called directly through a thin Python import launcher invoked by Bun. validate-receipt.mjs retains exact invocation; operations.jsonl retains arguments, cwd, stdout, stderr, times and child exitCode. Original helper exited 1 with three diagnostics: result revision or method differs from assignment; full coverage needs every required slice without gaps; each slice needs its assigned output. The Bun launcher itself exited 0 after recording the child failure, so child and outer exit statuses are distinct. No schema success, behavior success, or outcome grade is assigned. A green schema would not establish native behavior either.\n\n## Failures, limits and bounds\n\nRetained actual failures include sample's initial Bun syntax error, race-a missing local AGENTS.md read, race-b broad-output truncation followed by scoped reread, twice-missing method evidence, voluntary dropout, actual interrupt, and original helper diagnostics. parent-tool-returns.json preserves root-captured return objects where retained. Per-worker evidence files retain scoped raw tool arguments/returns; earliest source calls and full native collaboration message arguments remain native transcript only where no durable export was captured. native-cohort-1-spawns.json explicitly contains excerpts, not fabricated complete arguments. There is no complete exported raw transcript claim.\n\nLocal execution, case-owned writes and durable readback were authorized. External services/messages/forge mutations/publishing/schedules/config changes/installation/new commits were forbidden and not performed. Worker scopes and output directories remained distinct. Committed input bytes and Git HEAD remained unchanged. No real issue/PR or remote branch was created.\n\nThe 12-minute ceiling was tracked from pre-dispatch frame. At receipt-validation preparation, 516 seconds remained in case and 3045 seconds in batch. No ceiling was reached and no budget was reset. Open predicates remain complete required-slice coverage, accepted missing-method evidence, all-six simultaneous fanout, and independently observed model identities. No dependent retry continues after the one allowed attempt.\n"

      "path": "fixtures/cases/partition-and-race/outputs/source-read-return.json",
      "bytes": 17319,
      "sha256": "a70f17d3181d1ff68a921fbef62065b33c4fc22e2320a428f393edff353e912a",
      "owner": "local-013-initial-partition-and-race",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "{\"chunk_id\":\"60ea58\",\"wall_time_seconds\":0.000005041,\"exit_code\":0,\"original_token_count\":4260,\"output\":\"---\\nname: swarm\\ndescription: \\\"Fan out N parallel workers, drain them, and return one report. Use for /swarm, 'swarm this', or parallel coverage, races, gauntlets, and exploration.\\\"\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\n---\\n\\n# Swarm\\n\\nFan out N independent parallel workers. They may cover separate slices, race the same brief, or mix both. The parent waits, aggregates, and returns one report. Invocation metadata expresses upstream intent; do not assume the host enforces it.\\n\\nUse [session capabilities](../../references/host-capabilities.md), [model roles](../../references/model-roles.md), and the [assignment contract](../../references/orchestration-contract.md). Discover exposed operations and confirm authorization before dispatch. Upstream cloud/background execution and cloud branch selection require corresponding observed host bindings. Do not create remote branches or install capabilities as an incidental step.\\n\\n## Start\\n\\nOpen a task list with one entry per phase before launching anything. Use an in-chat list if no task-list tool is exposed.\\n\\n1. Frame\\n2. Fan out\\n3. Aggregate\\n4. Report\\n\\n## Phase A: Frame\\n\\n1. State the done predicate and the artifact or report the swarm must return.\\n2. Choose the shape. Partition into slices, race N workers on identical briefs, or mix both. For a race or mixed shape, declare `first pass`, `rank all`, or `best-of` before spawning.\\n3. Set N from the user or derive it from the shape. N is total workers, not the host concurrency limit.\\n4. Select the swarm worker model through current exposed model capabilities and the user's selection. The canonical configured/default model policy is host-specific; do not assume its model slug is supported here. For `auto` or `inherit-parent`, omit explicit model selection where the operation supports inheritance. Disclose any rejection and replacement. Use a supported same-family alternative when available and authorized, otherwise inherited selection; block an arm that requires an unavailable exact model. For a model race, name each arm's requested model up front and record actual models only when observed. Unknown actual models leave diversity unverified.\\n5. Give each worker its own writable output when it writes. When workers verify or measure commits, each brief names the exact full SHAs. A measurement brief also names the method, including sample count, what one sample is, and order. The worker records both in its result. Create immutable assignments and append-only attempt events under the assignment contract. Distinct output ownership applies even when race briefs are identical.\\n\\n## Phase B: Fan out\\n\\nDispatch all N assignments as one fan-out batch through exposed independent-worker operations, within actual concurrency limits. Prefer cloud/background execution when supported and authorized. Use local execution only when the worker needs something on the user's computer. If the host cannot provide the requested environment, disclose the limit before an authorized alternative. A non-default pushed branch requires an exposed base-branch binding and exact revision verification; never infer checkout from a branch label.\\n\\nEvery brief stands alone. Include the goal, scope, exact slice or race arm, how to verify, and what to report. Reports use `PASS`, `ISSUES`, or `BLOCKED` with evidence and links to existing artifacts. A worker that can prove a defect reports `ISSUES` and lists every issue it can prove, not only the first. Keep these report labels separate from assignment terminal states.\\n\\nIf a worker drops out, proceed with N-1 and note it. Record interrupted execution and retain the affected slice as a coverage gap unless another valid assignment covers it.\\n\\nUnavailable or denied independent execution blocks full swarm behavior. Authorized sequential work is reduced capability. Preserve slice briefs and evidence checks, but do not claim parallel workers, independent results, background execution or race parity.\\n\\n## Phase C: Aggregate\\n\\nWait for terminal results and inspect each result and its artifacts. Drop a result that does not record the SHAs and method its brief names, and respawn that worker once after inspecting the prior attempt and confirming permissions. Keep the same assignment identity, revision, scope and output ownership, and record the retry event. After a second miss, record a gap. A gap does not count as a pass. Missing artifacts and stale revisions also prevent acceptance; they do not become success through retry policy.\\n\\nFor coverage, every required slice needs a valid result. For a race, apply the selection rule declared up front. Use first pass, rank all, or best-of. Do not paste raw worker dumps. Selecting a winner does not hide gaps in required coverage.\\n\\nKeep a compact result table, one-line evidenced issues, and explicit gaps or dropouts. Cancel workers only through authorized exposed stop bindings. Inspect terminal state and quarantine partial artifacts from integration. If stopping cannot be observed, say termination is unverified. Cancellation never makes partial output a pass. Preserve durable assignment records with readback when available; missing persistence leaves recovery limits visible.\\n\\n## Phase D: Report\\n\\nReturn one consolidated in-chat report with the table, issue one-liners, gaps or dropouts, and the race rule when used. Include capability reductions, blocked assignments, fallback model selections, cancellation limits and unverified coverage. never the quality of a judgment.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    errors = []\\n    require_race = kind == 'swarm'\\n    kind = 'swarm' if kind == 'swarm-partition' else kind\\n    def require(condition, detail):\\n assigned input revision required')\\n        require(inspected(receipt['reviewArtifact']) and bool(receipt['reviewedBy'].strip()), 'qualitative outcome needs an explicit reviewed artifact')\\n        assignments = receipt['assignments']\\n        require(isinstance(assignments, list) and len(assignments) >= 2, 'independent assignments required')\\n        by_id, workers, outputs = {}, set(), []\\n        for a in assignments:\\n            keys = {'id','workerId','inputRevision','outputOwner','outputPath','role','status','startedOrder','completedOrder','requestedModel','actualModel','retryCount','method','taskArtifact','rubricProvided','resultRevision','resultMethod','outputSha256'}\\n            require(set(a) == keys, 'assignment fields differ from contract')\\n            require(a['id'] not in by_id and a['workerId'] not in workers, 'assignment and worker identities must be outputs.append(output)\\n            require(all(type(a[k]) is int for k in ('startedOrder','completedOrder','retryCount')) and type(receipt['declaredOrder']) is int, 'orders and retry count must be require(all(by_id[i]['role'] == 'partition' and by_id[i]['status'] == 'completed' for i in partition['results'].values()), 'required slices must complete')\\n            if race is None and not       "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "Reviewer /root inspected actual cohort 1 result files, complete method objects, exact revision fields, raw artifact pointers and independent SHA256 bytes. Each reports cache 1 and invoice 3 from four physically ordered records. Race rank-all examined both completed survivors; equivalent valid counts use predeclared race-a tie-break. Output digests differ because assignment metadata differ, not topic counts.\n\nMetadata-integrity worker actually omitted method/resultMethod twice, with two distinct immutable attempt files. Parent inspected and rejected both, one real followup retry only. No synthetic returned method was inserted. Its slice remains a gap. Dropout worker actually withdrew without coverage and returned blocked metadata; dropout-coverage remains gap. Cancellation worker wrote partial bytes, was interrupted through actual collaboration control, then list_agents observed interrupted. Partial artifact was copied byte-identically to quarantine and both paths excluded from integration. This is observed task interruption, not proof all possible OS processes terminated.\n\nTwo cohorts due three child slots do not establish all-six simultaneous fanout. Native concurrent race participants ran before any completion inspection. Requested inherited model, all actual models unknown. No diversity, timing performance, service success or full coverage claim. Initial source reads and some full native tool arguments remain in native transcript; durable captures explicitly identify their scope. Independent judging happens later.\n"

      "text": "{\"capturingOwner\":\"/root\",\"sourceRead\":{\"chunk_id\":\"60ea58\",\"wall_time_seconds\":0.000005041,\"exit_code\":0,\"original_token_count\":4260,\"output\":\"---\\nname: swarm\\ndescription: \\\"Fan out N parallel workers, drain them, and return one report. Use for /swarm, 'swarm this', or parallel coverage, races, gauntlets, and exploration.\\\"\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\n---\\n\\n# Swarm\\n\\nFan out N independent parallel workers. They may cover separate slices, race the same brief, or mix both. The parent waits, aggregates, and returns one report. Invocation metadata expresses upstream intent; do not assume the host enforces it.\\n\\nUse [session capabilities](../../references/host-capabilities.md), [model roles](../../references/model-roles.md), and the [assignment contract](../../references/orchestration-contract.md). Discover exposed operations and confirm authorization before dispatch. Upstream cloud/background execution and cloud branch selection require corresponding observed host bindings. Do not create remote branches or install capabilities as an incidental step.\\n\\n## Start\\n\\nOpen a task list with one entry per phase before launching anything. Use an in-chat list if no task-list tool is exposed.\\n\\n1. Frame\\n2. Fan out\\n3. Aggregate\\n4. Report\\n\\n## Phase A: Frame\\n\\n1. State the done predicate and the artifact or report the swarm must return.\\n2. Choose the shape. Partition into slices, race N workers on identical briefs, or mix both. For a race or mixed shape, declare `first pass`, `rank all`, or `best-of` before spawning.\\n3. Set N from the user or derive it from the shape. N is total workers, not the host concurrency limit.\\n4. Select the swarm worker model through current exposed model capabilities and the user's selection. The canonical configured/default model policy is host-specific; do not assume its model slug is supported here. For `auto` or `inherit-parent`, omit explicit model selection where the operation supports inheritance. Disclose any rejection and replacement. Use a supported same-family alternative when available and authorized, otherwise inherited selection; block an arm that requires an unavailable exact model. For a model race, name each arm's requested model up front and record actual models only when observed. Unknown actual models leave diversity unverified.\\n5. Give each worker its own writable output when it writes. When workers verify or measure commits, each brief names the exact full SHAs. A measurement brief also names the method, including sample count, what one sample is, and order. The worker records both in its result. Create immutable assignments and append-only attempt events under the assignment contract. Distinct output ownership applies even when race briefs are identical.\\n\\n## Phase B: Fan out\\n\\nDispatch all N assignments as one fan-out batch through exposed independent-worker operations, within actual concurrency limits. Prefer cloud/background execution when supported and authorized. Use local execution only when the worker needs something on the user's computer. If the host cannot provide the requested environment, disclose the limit before an authorized alternative. A non-default pushed branch requires an exposed base-branch binding and exact revision verification; never infer checkout from a branch label.\\n\\nEvery brief stands alone. Include the goal, scope, exact slice or race arm, how to verify, and what to report. Reports use `PASS`, `ISSUES`, or `BLOCKED` with evidence and links to existing artifacts. A worker that can prove a defect reports `ISSUES` and lists every issue it can prove, not only the first. Keep these report labels separate from assignment terminal states.\\n\\nIf a worker drops out, proceed with N-1 and note it. Record interrupted execution and retain the affected slice as a coverage gap unless another valid assignment covers it.\\n\\nUnavailable or denied independent execution blocks full swarm behavior. Authorized sequential work is reduced capability. Preserve slice briefs and evidence checks, but do not claim parallel workers, independent results, background execution or race parity.\\n\\n## Phase C: Aggregate\\n\\nWait for terminal results and inspect each result and its artifacts. Drop a result that does not record the SHAs and method its brief names, and respawn that worker once after inspecting the prior attempt and confirming permissions. Keep the same assignment identity, revision, scope and output ownership, and record the retry event. After a second miss, record a gap. A gap does not count as a pass. Missing artifacts and stale revisions also prevent acceptance; they do not become success through retry policy.\\n\\nFor coverage, every required slice needs a valid result. For a race, apply the selection rule declared up front. Use first pass, rank all, or best-of. Do not paste raw worker dumps. Selecting a winner does not hide gaps in required coverage.\\n\\nKeep a compact result table, one-line evidenced issues, and explicit gaps or dropouts. Cancel workers only through authorized exposed stop bindings. Inspect terminal state and quarantine partial artifacts from integration. If stopping cannot be observed, say termination is unverified. Cancellation never makes partial output a pass. Preserve durable assignment records with readback when available; missing persistence leaves recovery limits visible.\\n\\n## Phase D: Report\\n\\nReturn one consolidated in-chat report with the table, issue one-liners, gaps or dropouts, and the race rule when used. Include capability reductions, blocked assignments, fallback model selections, cancellation limits and unverified coverage. A source review or structural check never proves host behavior.\\ndef validate_method(record, kind, root, artifact, required_slice_count=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Check observable method relationships, never the quality of a judgment.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    errors = []\\n    require_race = kind == 'swarm'\\n    kind = 'swarm' if kind == 'swarm-partition' else kind\\n    def require(condition, detail):\\n        if not condition:\\n            errors.append('method receipt: ' + detail)\\n    def inspected(path):\\n        return path in record['artifacts'] and artifact(path)\\n    path = record.get('methodReceipt')\\n    if not inspected(path):\\n        return ['method receipt: existing listed receipt required']\\n    operations = record['successfulOperations']\\n    require(any(o['operation'] == 'inspect-method' and o['evidence'] == path for o in operations), 'receipt must be inspected')\\n    try:\\n        receipt = json.loads((root / path).read_text())\\n        common = {'schemaVersion', 'kind', 'inputRevision', 'assignments', 'reviewArtifact', 'reviewedBy', 'declaredOrder'}\\n        extra = {'candidates', 'judge', 'rubricArtifact', 'synthesis'} if kind == 'arena' else {'partition', 'race', 'cancelled', 'quarantined', 'failureExercises'}\\n        require(set(receipt) == common | extra and receipt['schemaVersion'] == 1 and receipt['kind'] == kind, 'invalid method shape or identity')\\n        require(re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{40}', record.get('inputRevision', '')) is not None and receipt['inputRevision'] == record.get('inputRevision'), 'exact assigned input revision required')\\n        require(inspected(receipt['reviewArtifact']) and bool(receipt['reviewedBy'].strip()), 'qualitative outcome needs an explicit reviewed artifact')\\n        assignments = receipt['assignments']\\n        require(isinstance(assignments, list) and len(assignments) >= 2, 'independent assignments required')\\n        by_id, workers, outputs = {}, set(), []\\n        for a in assignments:\\n            keys = {'id','workerId','inputRevision','outputOwner','outputPath','role','status','startedOrder','completedOrder','requestedModel','actualModel','retryCount','method','taskArtifact','rubricProvided','resultRevision','resultMethod','outputSha256'}\\n            require(set(a) == keys, 'assignment fields differ from contract')\\n            require(a['id'] not in by_id and a['workerId'] not in workers, 'assignment and worker identities must be require(a['outputOwner'] == a['workerId'], 'output owner differs from assigned worker')\\n            require(a['resultRevision'] == a['inputRevision'] and a['resultMethod'] == a['method'], 'result revision or method differs from assignment')\\n            output = (root / a['outputPath']).resolve()\\n            require(artifact(a['outputPath']) and __import__('hashlib').sha256(output.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == a['outputSha256'], 'output bytes differ from inspected result')\\n            require(inspected(a['outputPath']) and all(output != prior and not output.is_relative_to(prior.parent) and not prior.is_relative_to(output.parent) for prior in outputs), 'output ownership overlaps or artifact missing')\\n            outputs.append(output)\\n            require(all(type(a[k]) is int for k in ('startedOrder','completedOrder','retryCount')) and type(receipt['declaredOrder']) is int, 'orders and retry count must be require(a['status'] in ('completed','cancelled','blocked','failed'), 'invalid terminal state')\\n            require(all(isinstance(a[k], str) and a[k].strip() for k in method['sampleCount'] > 0 and bool(method['sampleDefinition'].strip()) and bool(method['order'].strip()), 'exact measurement method required')\\n            needed = ['spawn', 'inspect-result'] + ([] if a['role'] == 'judge' else ['isolated-write'])\\n            if a['status'] == 'cancelled': needed += ['stop','inspect-terminal','quarantine-artifacts']\\n            for op in needed:\\n                require(any(o.get('assignmentId') == a['id'] and o['operation'] == op and o['evidence'] == a['outputPath'] for o in operations), a['id'] + ': missing bound ' + op)\\n        require(record['permissions']['execution'] == 'granted' and record['permissions']['writes'] == 'granted', 'worker execution and output writes require grants')\\n        if kind == 'arena':\\n            ids = receipt['candidates']\\n            require(len(ids) >= 2 and len(set(ids)) == len(ids), 'at least two distinct candidates required')\\n            candidates = [by_id[i] for i in ids]\\n            judge = by_id[receipt['judge']]\\n            require(judge['id'] not in ids and judge['role'] == 'judge' and judge['status'] == 'completed', 'fresh completed judge required')\\n            require(judge['rubricProvided'] is True, 'judge must receive the rubric')\\n            require(all(a['role'] == 'candidate' and a['status'] == 'completed' and a['rubricProvided'] is False for a in candidates), 'completed candidates must not receive the rubric')\\n            require(len({a['taskArtifact'] for a in candidates}) == 1, 'candidates need the same task artifact')\\n            require(all(a['completedOrder'] < judge['startedOrder'] for a in candidates), 'judge started before candidates completed')\\n            require(inspected(receipt['rubricArtifact']), 'rubric evidence missing')\\n            synthesis = receipt['synthesis']\\n            require(set(synthesis) == {'order','inspectedCandidates','artifact','verificationArtifact'}, 'invalid synthesis shape')\\n            require(set(synthesis['inspectedCandidates']) == set(ids) and len(synthesis['inspectedCandidates']) == len(ids), 'parent must inspect every candidate')\\n            require(type(synthesis['order']) is int and synthesis['order'] > judge['completedOrder'] and inspected(synthesis['artifact']) and inspected(synthesis['verificationArtifact']), 'inspected synthesis and verification must follow judgment')\\n        else:\\n            partition, race = receipt['partition'], receipt['race']\\n            require(set(partition) == {'requiredSlices','results','gaps'}, 'partition shape invalid')\\n            slices = partition['requiredSlices']\\n            if required_slice_count is not None:\\n                require(len(slices) == required_slice_count, 'case requires exact declared slice count')\\n            require(bool(slices) and len(slices) == len(set(slices)) and set(partition['results']) == set(slices) and not partition['gaps'], 'full coverage needs every required slice without gaps')\\n            require(len(set(partition['results'].values())) == len(slices), 'each slice needs its assigned output')\\n            require(all(by_id[i]['role'] == 'partition' and by_id[i]['status'] == 'completed' for i in partition['results'].values()), 'required slices must complete')\\n            if race is None and not require_race:\\n                require(not receipt['cancelled'] and not receipt['quarantined'] and not receipt['failureExercises'], 'partition-only receipt cannot hide cancellation or failure exercises')\\n                return errors\\n            require(set(race) == {'rule','assignments','winner','ruleArtifact'}, 'race shape invalid')\\n            racers = [by_id[i] for i in race['assignments']]\\n            require(len(racers) >= 2 and len({a['id'] for a in racers}) == len(racers) and all(a['role'] == 'race' for a in racers), 'race needs distinct competing assignments')\\n            require(race['rule'] in ('first-pass','rank-all','best-of') and inspected(race['ruleArtifact']), 'declared race rule and inspected selection required')\\n            survivors = [a for a in racers if a['status'] == 'completed']\\n            require(bool(survivors) and race['winner'] in {a['id'] for a in survivors}, 'winner must be an inspected survivor')\\n            if race['rule'] == 'first-pass' and survivors:\\n                require(race['winner'] == min(survivors, key=lambda a: a['completedOrder'])['id'], 'first-pass winner violates completion order')\\n            if race['rule'] == 'rank-all': require(len(survivors) == len(racers), 'rank-all requires all race results')\\n            cancelled = {a['id'] for a in assignments if a['status'] == 'cancelled'}\\n            require(bool(cancelled) and set(receipt['cancelled']) == cancelled and set(receipt['quarantined']) == cancelled, 'cancellation exercise requires observed stop, terminal inspection and quarantine')\\n            exercises = receipt['failureExercises']\\n            rejected, dropouts = {}, set()\\n            require(isinstance(exercises,list), 'failure exercise records required')\\n            for event in exercises:\\n                require(set(event) == {'assignmentId','kind','attempt','order','reason','evidence'}, 'failure exercise shape invalid')\\n       "text": "{\n  \"registry\": {\n    \"caseId\": \"partition-and-race\",\n    \"caseFile\": \"docs/parity/cases/skills/swarm.json\",\n    \"sourcePaths\": [\n      \"skills/swarm/SKILL.md\"\n    ],\n    \"requiredHosts\": [\n      \"chatgpt\",\n      \"codex\"\n    ],\n    \"structuralOnly\": false,\n    \"hostStatus\": {\n      \"chatgpt\": \"not-run\",\n      \"codex\": \"not-run\"\n    },\n    \"requiredObservations\": {\n      \"pass\": [\n        \"read\",\n        \"inspect-result\"\n      ],\n      \"reduced-pass\": [\n        \"read\"\n      ]\n    },\n    \"executionModes\": {\n      \"pass\": [\n        \"workers\"\n      ],\n      \"reduced-pass\": [\n        \"direct\",\n        \"sequential\",\n        \"workers\"\n      ]\n    },\n    \"requiredPermissions\": {\n      \"pass\": [\n        \"execution\",\n        \"writes\"\n      ],\n      \"reduced-pass\": []\n    },\n    \"requiredMethod\": \"swarm\"\n  },\n  \"case\": {\n    \"id\": \"partition-and-race\",\n    \"sourcePaths\": [\n      \"skills/swarm/SKILL.md\"\n    ],\n    \"prompt\": \"Run a swarm with coverage slices and a race. Declare the slices and race selection rule before dispatch. Verify exact revisions with a declared sampling method; inject one result missing required evidence, a repeated miss, and a dropout, then cancel one assignment.\",\n    \"requirements\": [\n      \"Independent worker spawn, wait and result inspection when available and authorized\",\n      \"Exact SHA and measurement method in assignment and result\",\n      \"Distinct writable outputs and existing evidence artifacts\",\n      \"Stop, terminal inspection and artifact quarantine for cancellation\"\n    ],\n    \"expectedFull\": \"Declare each partition slice and first pass, rank all or best-of race rule before dispatch. Record exact SHAs and method including sample count, sample definition and order. Reject invalid evidence, retry the same assignment at most once and leave a second miss as a gap. Continue with N-1 after a dropout and disclose it. Aggregate coverage only with results for every required slice. Inspect artifacts and quarantine cancelled partial output; report table, evidenced issues, gaps, dropouts and the declared race rule.\",\n    \"expectedReduced\": \"Authorized sequential slices retain revisions, method, evidence checks and visible gaps, and explicitly report reduced capability without claiming independent workers, background execution, model diversity or race parity. Missing cancellation or persistence bindings remain visible.\",\n    \"expectedBlocked\": \"Unavailable or denied worker execution blocks full swarm behavior. Missing exact required model, source revision, artifact access or permission blocks the affected assignment. An unobserved stop leaves termination unverified and partial artifacts excluded.\",\n    \"forbiddenClaims\": [\n      \"Sequential work proves independent workers or race parity\",\n      \"Missing SHA, method, artifacts or coverage counts as PASS\",\n      \"More than one retry for invalid SHA or method evidence\",\n      \"Requested models prove actual models or diversity\",\n      \"Cancellation notification proves termination without terminal inspection\",\n      \"Unapproved external action or cloud setup is authorized by swarm\"\n    ]\n  },\n  \"sourceFiles\": [\n    \"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/skills/swarm/SKILL.md\"\n  ]\n}\n"
